# Excalidraw Data
## Text Elements
We can use the MSB to represent negative numbers.

There are three representations for signed binary

    - Sign magnitude representation
    - Ones complement representation (diminished radix complement or DRC)
    - Twos complement representation (radix complement or RC)

- Indicating the word size (eg. 6-bit or 8-bit) is important,
as the MSB is the sign bit (MSB = 0 indicates positive). ^wzGSJU0y

DRC - to get negatives, mirror the binary table vertically, and flip all the digits.
     - remember negative 0 exists, which would be 1111. 
     - negative 1 would be 1110. ^KJyTxpxf

[[Year 1]] ^qJYwk34Z
